# LOCKED FILES — DO NOT TOUCH WITHOUT EXPLICIT PERMISSION

## How This Works

- If a file is listed here as **LOCKED**, Claude MUST NOT edit it unless the user says "unlock X" or explicitly permits the edit in that message.
- Even then, confirm before touching.
- To lock a file: add a row below. To unlock: user says "unlock <file>" and row gets removed.
- Locks apply to BOTH local and server copies.

- **2026-07-21 (SWITCH_MATRIX_TRB tier-lie fixpack, HANDS_FREE):** Root-caused why every field in a "block" produced the SAME outcome (user's `WT_DC_ENTRY_THRESHOLD 35/43/55/75` example). THREE defects, all fixed (backups `backups/before_matrix_fixpack_*_202607211940.py`, all synced S1): **(1) TIER-MIXING LIE** — 21,937 of 25,600 tradier `param_cells` were Tier-1 vec screens stored identically to Tier-2 engine truth; 16,880 carried a delta subtracted from a Tier-2 ENGINE baseline (a tier gap, not a knob effect → MU_LONG showed a phantom +1.3917%/mo on 495 cells). Fix: `param_cells` gains `tier/baseline_stamp/trades_fingerprint/inert/delta_legacy_crosstier` cols; `vec_screen_daemon` now builds its OWN Tier-1 baseline (campaign `..__vec`) and diffs same-tier only; `export_switch_matrix_xls.py --tier {ENGINE,VEC}` never mixes tiers (VEC → `SWITCH_MATRIX_TRB_VEC_DIAGNOSTIC`). `tools/repair_tier_deltas.py` stamped all 26k historical rows + recomputed deltas same-tier + preserved originals in `delta_legacy_crosstier` (nothing deleted). **(2) DEAD/DEGENERATE knobs surfaced** — new `status` col: OK / INERT_AT_VALUE / RECONNECT (every value bit-identical to baseline = knob unread) / DEGENERATE (values differ from baseline but not each other = saturates or vec/live default mismatch, e.g. WT_DC_ENTRY_THRESHOLD vec-default 0.0 vs live 45) / PENDING. Trade-list fingerprint makes "changed nothing" mechanical, not eyeballed. **(3) THROUGHPUT** — Tier-2 lane was CRASH-LOOPED DEAD (all 10 workers exited on uncaught `database is locked` from `insert_cell`; 31 crashes/worker) AND `combo_search.py` had held the shared sqlite DB **5h06m with 0 log progress**, starving every writer while the old 30-try commit loop silently discarded 9-engine-minute units. Fix: `param_results_store.busy_retry()` wraps all writes; per-unit try/except so one bad unit never kills a worker; commit-fail now REPORTED not swallowed; `param_matrix_daemon --all-tiers` measures VEC-tier params with the real engine for keys that must be complete; watchdog gains a combo_search hang-guard (kill if log stale >60min) + `--all-tiers`. Three MORE throughput bugs found while verifying the relaunch: (a) `connect()` ran DDL on EVERY connection, taking a write lock each time — a 14-worker fleet starved itself at startup (workers stuck at 1s CPU for 20+ min, never reaching the work loop); now `_schema_current()` checks read-only first (connect: minutes -> 0.00s). (b) `release_dead_claims()` — claims are `pmx:<tag>:<pid>` honoured 1800s, so a killed generation locked the queue 30min after every restart (489 stale claims measured). (c) `psc.free_mb()` reads AVAILABLE RAM (not disk) and `run_symbol` blocks below 8GB — explains "workers alive, nothing running". One MU Tier-2 run measured = 9m11s / 632MB RSS (CPU-bound, not the assumed 10.8GB). VERIFIED PRODUCING: 14 workers `--only MU --all-tiers`, 37 engine procs, MU_LONG ENGINE cells 156->179. **PHASE PLAN (USER 2026-07-21 evening):** first four keys = **MU_LONG, HAO_SHORT, NVDA_LONG, VT_LONG**, worked **one ticker at a time** (all 14 workers on the same key). `tools/matrix_focus.py` holds the focus in `data/matrix_focus.json` and only advances when the current key's grid is >=99.5% complete; the watchdog reads it and kills any worker pinned to a stale ticker. `combo_search.py --plan MU:LONG,HAO:SHORT,NVDA:LONG,VT:LONG` runs the "hard part" (combining switches) on the same key. `tools/param_shortlist.py` (NEW) is the phase-2 decision: from the pilot keys it ranks every switch PROMOTE / WATCH / DEGENERATE / NO_EFFECT / INCOMPLETE so only PROMOTE switches get swept across all 129 tradeable keys. **Batching symbols into one engine run was measured and REJECTED**: 4 symbols in one invocation = ~29min vs ~36.7min for 4 singles (only ~20%), at 1.4GB RSS vs 632MB, and one invocation shares capital/position slots/cross-symbol ranking so per-symbol trades would not match the single-symbol baselines every existing cell uses; `run_symbols()` removed, rationale kept as a comment in persym_baseline_campaign.py. Also fixed my own over-aggressive combo hang-guard (a stale log alone is NOT a hang — combo only logs after each 9-15min probe; it killed 5 legitimate runs. Now requires log stale >2h AND no engine child). DO NOT revert. Tools only — no live trading code touched.

| `ez_market_data.py` | RELOCKED 2026-07-02 ws-combined-stream-unwrap | **2026-07-02 USER ("unlock and fix ez_market_data").** WS combined-stream parse fix. `ws_injector` uses the combined endpoint `wss://fstream.binance.com/market/stream?streams=!markPrice@arr@1s` (the raw `/ws/!markPrice@arr` path silently sends 0 msgs since the 2026-05-10 Binance change). Combined streams wrap EVERY payload as `{"stream":"...","data":[ ... ]}` (a **dict**), but line ~475 checked `isinstance(data, list)` directly → the dict never matched → **every mark-price msg silently dropped** → all ~204 crypto prices froze while the WS still logged "🔌 Connected" (froze ~9.75h on 2026-07-01). FIX (additive, 3 lines before the list check): `if isinstance(data, dict) and isinstance(data.get('data'), (list, dict)): data = data['data']` — unwraps the envelope so the inner array is processed; preserves the raw-list path. VERIFIED LIVE: direct WS probe confirmed dict{'stream','data'=list[118-736]}; post-restart stale-symbol count dropped ~200 → ~6 (transient 3s-threshold jitter, mostly symbols absent from stream). Backup `backups/before_ws_combined_stream_unwrap_202607021506.py`. ROLLBACK: remove the 3-line dict-unwrap. **2026-07-03 ADDED (2nd fix, same session): WS data-liveness self-heal reconnect.** A long-lived connection to fstream gets SILENTLY throttled (this IP is not whitelisted at Binance) — data frames stop but ping/pong keeps the socket alive so `async for msg in ws` blocks forever and prices freeze for hours with no reconnect (observed 2026-07-03: connection delivered until 01:03:40 then silent 88s+ while a fresh connect got 736 msgs in 2s). FIX: replaced `async for msg in ws` with a bounded loop — `msg = await asyncio.wait_for(ws.receive(), timeout=10)` + a monotonic `_last_data` guard that breaks to reconnect when no TEXT data for 20s (ping/pong frames reset a frame-timeout but NOT this data-timeout). Verified LIVE: self-heal fires every ~2-3min under throttle and reconnects; bounds the freeze from HOURS to ~1-2min cycles (15 reconnects/3h, controlled — not a thrash storm). RESIDUAL: ~2-min staleness cycles remain because Binance throttles this IP's sustained connections + slows its reconnects — **that is the IP-whitelist INFRA issue (IP 84.26.62.192 not whitelisted at Binance), NOT a code bug; no code change fully fixes it.** New md5: **620fdb9a94dd3e34da4dccc1e233ae3e** (Mac; sync S1 via push.py). Backup `backups/before_ws_dataliveness_reconnect_2026070*.py`. ROLLBACK (self-heal only): restore `async for msg in ws:` loop. Prior lock (WT 1m/3m hot_metrics + klines_cache_writeback 60s cycle) preserved — untouched. DO NOT revert without explicit user authorisation. | user |

## Rules For Claude When This File Exists

1. **Before editing ANY file** — check this list.
2. If the file is in the **Locked** table → STOP. Tell the user the file is locked and ask for explicit unlock.
3. If a fix to a Hot Zone file would require touching a Locked file → raise it explicitly, get permission first.
4. When a Hot Zone file is confirmed stable and the user says "lock it" → add it to the Locked table immediately.
5. Never silently edit a locked file "just this one import" or "just one line" — the lock is absolute.
